| D1 | 2026-10-04 | **User instruction for this task:** "persiapkan mulai plan dan spec, dan lanjutkan implementasi full autonomous dengan mencatat semua temuan, drift dan keputusan yang kamu ambil." | D5 | 2026-10-04 | **PostgreSQL on this host (U3).** The request cannot be met without a PostgreSQL server, so provisioning one is taken as authorised by the request itself, in the least persistent form: no Docker (U1), no login/background service and no shell-profile edit (U2), loopback only (U4), project-local data directory that is git-ignored, credentials generated locally and git-ignored, a non-default port so nothing else on the host is touched. Order of preference, to be settled by research: (1) real PostgreSQL binaries distributed through npm and started by project scripts (no host install at all, and a candidate for a portable live package); (2) Homebrew `postgresql@17` keg, started with `pg_ctl` by project scripts. Either is removable with one command (`npm uninstall` / `brew uninstall` + deleting the data directory) | Controller under D1; RT item U3 | Initialization step provisions PostgreSQL; the final choice is recorded after research |

biometric unlock by a server-issued single-use rotating token (no session from a client's claim); idle activity = POST requests and `touchSession` only; realtime with 5 topics, gap-free revisions, close codes 4401 / 4403 / 4408; last-administrator rule (409 `LAST_ADMIN`); health `service` widened additively; migrations applied by a command under the owner role while the runtime role only checks the schema version (follows TD-02 over TD-04's wording); Content: **29 tables** for the 25 stored entities (three entities split in two, plus `audit_chain_head` and `schema_meta`), 292 columns, 24 foreign keys all `ON DELETE RESTRICT`, 93 named CHECKs; time is `bigint` epoch milliseconds; value sets are `text` with CHECKs (vocabulary checked by format so additive types need no migration); password as separate Argon2id columns in its own table; session token, device credential and biometric token stored only as SHA-256 `bytea` with unique-index lookup; pairing code hashed with its code ID and compared in the server; never-log list NL-1..NL-8; **audit append-only as a database feature** (`dems_app` has INSERT and SELECT only, a statement-level trigger refuses UPDATE / DELETE / TRUNCATE for every role, a single-row `audit_chain_head` locked `FOR UPDATE` serialises the chain); privilege matrix and boot checks B-1..B-8; four migration files in fixed order; reset routine R-0..R-9; three template databases with one clone per test file; no PostgreSQL extension assumed. **Rulings on SPEC-GAP-1..7 — the contract's defaults are adopted:** (1) one string rule in the contract registry rejects U+0000 and ill-formed UTF-16 in free text; (2) pairing codes and the device / code counters are kept across a reset; (3) an ACTIVE code at reset time is written `CANCELLED`; (4) the health snapshot gets the 17 columns chosen in the contract; (5) the realtime epoch continues as previous + 1 after a reset; (6) `lastSignInAt` after a reset = the newest kept session's issue time; (7) several open sessions per device are allowed, the registry shows the newest. **Refinements accepted:** reset order = build, carry over, swap, drop the old database last (amends TD-04's wording — dropping first would lose kept sessions and devices on a later failure); the hardware snapshot is one row with one column per slice (amends ADQ-4's "one JSON row"); test database names `dems_test_step_<N>_<stamp>_…`. | D16 | 2026-10-04 | **Security design review (row 20): verdict CAUTION — 0 Critical, 0 High, 5 Medium, 18 Low, 6 Info; no finding needs a U1–U9 action. Controller choice under D1: FIX NOW — all 24 corrections of review §7 are folded into the design documents before any dependent implementation row starts.** The five Medium: SD-01 the server must not connect as the cluster superuser — a non-superuser role `dems_owner` (CREATEDB) owns the databases and does migrate / seed / reset / tests, `dems_admin` is used only at provisioning (closes F23 GD-02; amends the "two roles" of D11); SD-02 two Origin lists (web console origins for the cookie and web sign-in; the server's own origin only for requests without a cookie) so pairing cannot be bypassed by an own-origin request; SD-03 a denial budget at the throttle stage (20 refusals per key per 60 s, then 429) so `ACCESS_DENIED` rows cannot flood the audit trail, while every refusal within the budget is still audited; SD-04 server-native state changes are atomic — one unit of work, the attempt consumed before verification, compare-and-set transitions, five concurrency tests (keeps the pairing-code guessing bound at 5 in 1,000,000 per code); SD-05 the request log holds the route pattern, never the raw URL, and parse errors are logged by code. **Other rulings:** GD-01 accepted as is (the database checks the chain link; the digest is checked by `verifyAuditChain`); the loopback cookie without `Secure` / `__Host-` is accepted and lapses if a listener ever leaves loopback or TLS is added; **the published demo password in Live mode is accepted only while every listener is on loopback** (conditions C1–C4; any network exposure must first replace the seeded password); DEPARTURE-6 accepted with its conditions (the fallback without `requireAuthentication` only on emulators / debug builds, no silent fallback, truthful wording); `@fastify/helmet` 13.1.1 adopted plus `Cache-Control: no-store`; `TOKEN_REUSE` added to the session end reasons; both audit triggers `ENABLE ALWAYS`; the Origin allowlist drops `http://localhost` (another project on this host listens on `[::1]:5173`); the cookie name is per instance (lanes share the 127.0.0.1 cookie jar); API Reference Try-it gets a deny list for the state-changing operations named in SD-16. **SD-19 (controller's choice):** the lineage of discarded audit chains is kept in a database table that survives every reset (head hash, row count, time, actor, reason per discarded chain), not in a file — it is queryable, covered by the same privileges and carried over by the reset routine. 17 residual risks accepted (review §8) | security-review row 20 (gateway_check/03_security_design_review.md);
